# 2913 new apps — 2026-11-25

**Anomaly:** 2913 new apps is 274% of the median of the last 7 reports (1065). Check for duplicates or a change in what Apple returns.

Window: apps first found between 2026-11-24 21:18 UTC (22:18 Europe/Warsaw) and 2026-11-25 21:01 UTC (22:01 Europe/Warsaw) and released since 2026-11-23 21:18 UTC (22:18 Europe/Warsaw), plus apps found earlier whose release date arrived in that span. Date-only release dates count by calendar day in America/Los_Angeles. Storefronts: us.
